# Retail 12.1.0 supplemental wikitext triage

Static snapshot: 2026-10-04. Source revision 6886719; source identities and annotations live in `data/patch-api/sources/12.1.0-wikitext-register.json`. Every appended ledger row remains `audit-pending`, with no capabilities or acceptance credit.

Method: Python exact-token text search over src/ plus cached retail Lua/API documentation; select registration/definition, not call sites or tests. `rg` unavailable; scratch search.py performs equivalent read-only text search. Namespace/file checks are mechanical, not a full reachability analysis. `retail/AddOns/` citations resolve under `~/.cache/wow-ui-sim/blizzard-ui/`; this cache is not authenticated build 69587 evidence. Other-profile files, shared method names, wrappers, gating, generated surfaces and native VM globals require runtime proof. Static absence is provisional, never claimed runtime proof.

Prior cross-references: `data/patch-api/sources/12.1-framexml.json` (320 added/112 removed), `data/patch-api/12.1-framexml.json` (PTR machine statuses), `data/patch-api/sources/12.1-behaviors.json` (54 behavior occurrences), `data/patch-api/12.1-behaviors.json` (PTR machine statuses). These provide history only. Aura/security/service behavior rows are not equivalent to these symbol occurrences; none of their statuses counts as Retail proof.
